buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});   const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n    \n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n    \n    // Initialize all elements with default material and world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic_swap[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic_swap[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n\n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    // (each iteration of x writes a disjoint set of  // Energy transfer coefficient\n    val_t external_flow;   // External energy source/sink\n};\n\n// Static connectivity information for each element\nstruct ElementStatic {\n    idx_t material_idx;\n    idx_t num_connections;\n    idx_t connected_idx[MAX_CONNECTIONS];     // Indices of connected elements\n    val_t connected_flux[MAX_CONNECTIONS];    // Flux coefficients for each connection\n};\n\n// Dynamic state for each element\nstruct ElementDynamic {\n    val_t current_energy;\n    val_t total_flux;\n};\n\n// World state\nstruct World {\n    std::vector<Material> materials;\n    std::vector<ElementStatic> elements_static;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic> elements_dynamic_swap;\n};\n\n// Material type IDs\nconstexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;\nconstexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;\nconstexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\n\n// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n    \n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n    \n    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy.\n    // Parallel first-touch with static scheduling so pages land on the NUMA\n    // nodes of the threads that will later process them in runSimulation.\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic_swap[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic_swap[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n\n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    // (each iteration of x writes a disjoint set of elements)\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {\n        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {\n            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];\n            \n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n            \n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n                \n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\ninline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,\n                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {\n    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) * \n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n    const ElementStatic* const elems_static = world.elements_static.data();\n    const Material* const materials = world.materials.data();\n    ElementDynamic* read_buf = world.elements_dynamic.data();\n    ElementDynamic* write_buf = world.elements_dynamic_swap.data();\n\n    // Single parallel region for all iterations to avoid per-iteration\n    // fork/join overhead. Each thread swaps its private buffer pointers after\n    // the implicit barrier of the worksharing loop, so no extra sync is needed.\n    #pragma omp parallel firstprivate(read_buf, write_buf)\n    {\n        for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n            // Update all elements\n            #pragma omp for schedule(static)\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n                const ElementStatic& elem_static = elems_static[i];\n                const ElementDynamic& elem_dyn = read_buf[i];\n                const Material& mat = materials[elem_static.material_idx];\n\n                // Start with external flow\n                val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;\n\n                // Add flux from all connected elements\n                for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {\n                    const idx_t neighbor_idx = elem_static.connected_idx[j];\n                    const ElementDynamic& neighbor_dyn = read_buf[neighbor_idx];\n                    total_flux += computeFlux(mat, elem_dyn, elem_static.connected_flux[j], neighbor_dyn);\n                }\n\n                // Update element state\n                ElementDynamic& elem_write = write_buf[i];\n                elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n                elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);\n            }\n            // Swap buffers (thread-private pointer copies)\n            std::swap(read_buf, write_buf);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Make world.elements_dynamic hold the final state, as before\n    if (n_iters % 2 != 0) {\n        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);\n    }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    \n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", energy_min, energy_max);\n    \n    // Check for numerical issues\n    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification\nuint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);\n        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Grid size (NxN elements) (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num>     Number of simulation iterations (default: 10)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -v           Enable validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -r           Print results for external validation\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -h           Show this help message\\n\");\n}\n\nint main(int argc, char** argv) {\n    int n_elems_root = 512;\n    int n_iters = 10;\n    bool validate = false;\n    bool printResults = false;\n    \n    // Parse command line arguments\n    for (int i = 1; i < argc; ++i) {\n        if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-n\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            n_elems_root = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-i\") == 0 && i + 1 < argc) {\n            n_iters = atoi(argv[++i]);\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-v\") == 0) {\n            validate = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-r\") == 0) {\n            printResults = true;\n        } else if (strcmp(argv[i], \"-h\") == 0) {\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 0;\n        } else {\n            printf(\"Unknown option: %s\\n\", argv[i]);\n            printUsage(argv[0]);\n            return 1;\n        }\n    }\n    \n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    printf(\"Unstructured Mesh Energy Transfer Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"============================================\\n\");\n    printf(\"Grid size: %d x %d = %d elements\\n\", n_elems_root, n_elems_root, n_elems);\n    printf(\"Iterations: %d\\n\", n_iters);\n    printf(\"Validation: %s\\n\", validate ? \"enabled\" : \"disabled\");\n    printf(\"\\n\");\n    \n    // Build the unstructured mesh\n    printf(\"Building unstructured mesh...\\n\");\n    World world;\n    buildSquare2D(world, n_elems_root);\n    \n    // Calculate memory usage\n    const size_t static_mem = This keeps vector::resize from\n// touching the memory on the main thread, so the pages are first touched by\n// the parallel initialization loop and placed NUMA-locally to the threads\n// that later process them.\ntemplate <typename T>\nstruct FirstTouchAllocator : std::allocator<T> {\n    template <typename U>\n    struct rebind { using other = FirstTouchAllocator<U>; };\n\n    template <typename U>\n    void construct(U* p) noexcept(noexcept(::new (static_cast<void*>(p)) U)) {\n        ::new (static_cast<void*>(p)) U;\n    }\n    template <typename U, typename... Args>\n    void construct(U* p, Args&&... args) {\n        ::new (static_cast<void*>(p)) U(std::forward<Args>(args)...);\n    }\n};\n\n// World state\nstruct World {\n    std::vector<Material> materials;\n    std::vector<ElementStatic, FirstTouchAllocator<ElementStatic>> elements_static;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic, FirstTouchAllocator<ElementDynamic>> elements_dynamic;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic, FirstTouchAllocator<ElementDynamic>> elements_dynamic_swap;\n};\n\n// Material type IDs\nconstexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;\nconstexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;\nconstexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\n\n// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0}); i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic_swap[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic_swap[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n\n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    // (each iteration of x writes a disjoint set of elements)\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {\n        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {\n            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];\n            \n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n            \n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n                \n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\ninline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,\n                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {\n    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) * \n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n    const ElementStatic* const elems_static = world.elements_static.data();\n    const Material* const materials = world.materials.data();\n    ElementDynamic* read_buf = world.elements_dynamic.data();\n    ElementDynamic* write_buf = world.elements_dynamic_swap.data();\n\n    // Single parallel region for all iterations to avoid per-iteration\n    // fork/join overhead. elem_write = write_buf[i];\n                elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n                elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);\n before\n    if (n_iters % 2 != 0) {\n        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);\n    }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification\nuint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic>& elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);\n        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s This keeps vector::resize from\n// touching the memory on the main thread, so the pages are first touched by\n// the parallel initialization loop and placed NUMA-locally to the threads\n// that later process them.\ntemplate <typename T>\nstruct FirstTouchAllocator : std::allocator<T> {\n    template <typename U>\n    struct rebind { using other = FirstTouchAllocator<U>; };\n\n    template <typename U>\n    void construct(U* p) noexcept(noexcept(::new (static_cast<void*>(p)) U)) {\n        ::new (static_cast<void*>(p)) U;\n    }\n    template <typename U, typename... Args>\n    void construct(U* p, Args&&... args) {\n        ::new (static_cast<void*>(p)) U(std::forward<Args>(args)...);\n    }\n};\n\n// World state\nstruct World {\n    std::vector<Material> materials;\n    std::vector<ElementStatic, FirstTouchAllocator<ElementStatic>> elements_static;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic, FirstTouchAllocator<ElementDynamic>> elements_dynamic;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic, FirstTouchAllocator<ElementDynamic>> elements_dynamic_swap;\n};\n\n// Material type IDs\nconstexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;\nconstexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;\nconstexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\n\n// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0});    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n    \n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n    \n    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy.\n    // Parallel first-touch with static scheduling so pages land on the NUMA\n    // nodes of the threads that will later process them in runSimulation.\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic_swap[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic_swap[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n\n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    // (each iteration of x writes a disjoint set of elements)\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static)\n    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {\n        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {\n            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];\n            \n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n            \n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n                \n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\ninline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,\n                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {\n    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) * \n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n    const ElementStatic* const elems_static = world.elements_static.data();\n    const Material* const materials = world.materials.data();\n    ElementDynamic* read_buf = world.elements_dynamic.data();\n    ElementDynamic* write_buf = world.elements_dynamic_swap.data();\n\n    // Single parallel region for all iterations to avoid per-iteration\n    // fork/join overhead. Each thread swaps its private buffer pointers after\n    // the implicit barrier of the worksharing loop, so no extra sync is needed.\n    #pragma omp parallel firstprivate(read_buf, write_buf)\n    {\n        for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n            // Update all elements\n            #pragma omp for schedule(static)\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n                const ElementStatic& elem_static = elems_static[i];\n                const ElementDynamic& elem_dyn = read_buf[i];\n                const Material& mat = materials[elem_static.material_idx];\n\n                // Start with external flow\n                val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;\n\n                // Add flux from all connected elements\n                for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {\n                    const idx_t neighbor_idx = elem_static.connected_idx[j];\n                    const ElementDynamic& neighbor_dyn = read_buf[neighbor_idx];\n                    total_flux += computeFlux(mat, elem_dyn, elem_static.connected_flux[j], neighbor_dyn);\n                }\n\n                // Update element state\n                ElementDynamic& elem_write = write_buf[i];\n                elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n                elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);\n            }\n            // Swap buffers (thread-private pointer copies)\n            std::swap(read_buf, write_buf);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Make world.elements_dynamic hold the final state, as before\n    if (n_iters % 2 != 0) {\n        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);\n    }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    \n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", energy_min, energy_max);\n    \n    // Check for numerical issues\n    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification\nuint64_t This keeps vector::resize from\n// touching the memory on the main thread, so the pages are first touched by\n// the parallel initialization loop and placed NUMA-locally to the threads\n// that later process them.\ntemplate <typename T>\nstruct FirstTouchAllocator : std::allocator<T> {\n    template <typename U>\n    struct rebind { using other = FirstTouchAllocator<U>; };\n\n    template <typename U>\n    void construct(U* p) noexcept(noexcept(::new (static_cast<void*>(p)) U)) {\n        ::new (static_cast<void*>(p)) U;\n    }\n    template <typename U, typename... Args>\n    void construct(U* p, Args&&... args) {\n        ::new (static_cast<void*>(p)) U(std::forward<Args>(args)...);\n    }\n};\n\n// World state\nstruct World {\n    std::vector<Material> materials;\n    std::vector<ElementStatic, FirstTouchAllocator<ElementStatic>> elements_static;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic, FirstTouchAllocator<ElementDynamic>> elements_dynamic;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic, FirstTouchAllocator<ElementDynamic>> elements_dynamic_swap;\n};\n\n// Material type IDs\nconstexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;\nconstexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;\nconstexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\n\n// Build a 2D square grid as an unstructured mesh\n// This represents computation on arbitrarily-shaped geometries\nvoid buildSquare2D(World& world, const int n_elems_root) {\n    const int n_elems = n_elems_root * n_elems_root;\n    \n    // Initialize materials\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.0}); x < n_elems_root; ++x) {\n        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {\n            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];\n            \n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n            \n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n                \n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\ninline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,\n                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {\n    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) * \n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n    const ElementStatic* const elems_static = world.elements_static.data();\n    const Material* const materials = world.materials.data();\n    ElementDynamic* read_buf = world.elements_dynamic.data();\n    ElementDynamic* write_buf = world.elements_dynamic_swap.data();\n\n    // Single parallel region for all iterations to avoid per-iteration\n    // fork/join overhead. before\n    if (n_iters % 2 != 0) {\n        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);\n    }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification\nuint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic, FirstTouchAllocator<ElementDynamic>>& elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Grid size (NxN elements) (default: 512)\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -i <num> printf(\"Unstructured Mesh Energy Transfer Benchmark\\n\");\n    printf(\"============================================\\n\");\n    printf(\"Grid size: %d x %d = %d elements\\n\", n_elems_root,    // Default material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, 0.5});    // Inflow material\n    world.materials.emplace_back(Material{0.8, -0.5});   // Outflow material\n    \n    // Allocate elements\n    world.elements_static.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic.resize(n_elems);\n    world.elements_dynamic_swap.resize(n_elems);\n    \n    // Initialize all elements with default material and zero energy.\n    // Parallel first-touch with static scheduling so pages land on the NUMA\n    // nodes of the threads that will later process them in runSimulation.\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) proc_bind(spread)\n    for (int i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n        world.elements_static[i].material_idx = DEFAULT_MAT_ID;\n        world.elements_static[i].num_connections = 0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic_swap[i].current_energy = 0.0;\n        world.elements_dynamic_swap[i].total_flux = 0.0;\n    }\n\n    // Build connectivity: each element connects to its neighbors in 2D grid\n    // (each iteration of x writes a disjoint set of elements)\n    #pragma omp parallel for schedule(static) proc_bind(spread)\n    for (int x = 0; x < n_elems_root; ++x) {\n        for (int y = 0; y < n_elems_root; ++y) {\n            const int idx = x * n_elems_root + y;\n            ElementStatic& elem = world.elements_static[idx];\n            \n            // Connect to neighbors (up, down, left, right)\n            const int offsets[4][2] = {{1, 0}, {-1, 0}, {0, 1}, {0, -1}};\n            \n            for (int n = 0; n < 4; ++n) {\n                const int nx = x + offsets[n][0];\n                const int ny = y + offsets[n][1];\n                \n                // Check if neighbor is within bounds\n                if (nx >= 0 && nx < n_elems_root && ny >= 0 && ny < n_elems_root) {\n                    const int neighbor_idx = nx * n_elems_root + ny;\n                    elem.connected_idx[elem.num_connections] = neighbor_idx;\n                    elem.connected_flux[elem.num_connections] = 1.0;\n                    elem.num_connections++;\n                }\n            }\n        }\n    }\n    \n    // Set corner elements as inflow/outflow to create interesting dynamics\n    const int last = n_elems_root - 1;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[0 * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + 0].material_idx = OUTFLOW_MAT_ID;\n    world.elements_static[last * n_elems_root + last].material_idx = INFLOW_MAT_ID;\n}\n\n// Compute energy flux between two elements\ninline val_t computeFlux(const Material& mat, const ElementDynamic& this_elem,\n                        val_t connection_flux, const ElementDynamic& other_elem) {\n    return (other_elem.current_energy - this_elem.current_energy) * \n           mat.transfer_coeff * connection_flux * 0.25;\n}\n\n// Run simulation for n_iters iterations\nvoid runSimulation(World& world, const int n_iters) {\n    const size_t n_elems = world.elements_static.size();\n    const ElementStatic* const elems_static = world.elements_static.data();\n    const Material* const materials = world.materials.data();\n    ElementDynamic* read_buf = world.elements_dynamic.data();\n    ElementDynamic* write_buf = world.elements_dynamic_swap.data();\n\n    // Single parallel region for all iterations to avoid per-iteration\n    // fork/join overhead. Each thread swaps its private buffer pointers after\n    // the implicit barrier of the worksharing loop, so no extra sync is needed.\n    #pragma omp parallel firstprivate(read_buf, write_buf) proc_bind(spread)\n    {\n        for (int iter = 0; iter < n_iters; ++iter) {\n            // Update all elements\n            #pragma omp for schedule(static)\n            for (size_t i = 0; i < n_elems; ++i) {\n                const ElementStatic& elem_static = elems_static[i];\n                const ElementDynamic& elem_dyn = read_buf[i];\n                const Material& mat = materials[elem_static.material_idx];\n\n                // Start with external flow\n                val_t total_flux = mat.external_flow;\n\n                // Add flux from all connected elements\n                for (idx_t j = 0; j < elem_static.num_connections; ++j) {\n                    const idx_t neighbor_idx = elem_static.connected_idx[j];\n                    const ElementDynamic& neighbor_dyn = read_buf[neighbor_idx];\n                    total_flux += computeFlux(mat, elem_dyn, elem_static.connected_flux[j], neighbor_dyn);\n                }\n\n                // Update element state\n                ElementDynamic& elem_write = write_buf[i];\n                elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n                elem_write.total_flux = elem_dyn.total_flux + std::abs(total_flux);\n            }\n            // Swap buffers (thread-private pointer copies)\n            std::swap(read_buf, write_buf);\n        }\n    }\n\n    // Make world.elements_dynamic hold the final state, as before\n    if (n_iters % 2 != 0) {\n        std::swap(world.elements_dynamic, world.elements_dynamic_swap);\n    }\n}\n\n// Validate simulation results\nbool validateResults(const World& world) {\n    val_t energy_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t flux_sum = 0.0;\n    val_t energy_max = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::lowest();\n    val_t energy_min = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    \n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = std::max(elem.current_energy, energy_max);\n        energy_min = std::min(elem.current_energy, energy_min);\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"Validation results:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  Energy sum: %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", energy_min, energy_max);\n    \n    // Check for numerical issues\n    constexpr val_t energy_epsilon = 1e-8;\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (std::abs(energy_sum) > energy_epsilon) {\n        printf(\"  WARNING: Energy sum diverged from 0 (expected conservation)\\n\");\n        // Don't fail validation as this can happen with external flows\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(flux_sum)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Flux sum is not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    if (!std::isfinite(energy_max) || !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification\nuint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic, FirstTouchAllocator<ElementDynamic>>& elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);\n        const uint64_t* f_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].total_flux);\n        hash ^= (*e_ptr + i) * 0x9e3779b97f4a7c15ULL;\n        hash ^= (*f_ptr + i) * 0xbf58476d1ce4e5b9ULL;\n    }\n    return hash;\n}\n\nvoid printUsage(const char* progName) {\n    printf(\"Usage: %s [options]\\n\", progName);\n    printf(\"Options:\\n\");\n    printf(\"  -n <num>     Grid size (NxN elements) This keeps vector::resize from\n// touching the memory on the main thread, so the pages are first touched by\n// the parallel initialization loop and placed NUMA-locally to the threads\n// that later process them.\ntemplate <typename T>\nstruct FirstTouchAllocator : std::allocator<T> {\n    template <typename U>\n    struct rebind { using other = FirstTouchAllocator<U>; };\n\n    template <typename U>\n    void construct(U* p) noexcept(noexcept(::new (static_cast<void*>(p)) U)) {\n        ::new (static_cast<void*>(p)) U;\n    }\n    template <typename U, typename... Args>\n    void construct(U* p, Args&&... args) {\n        ::new (static_cast<void*>(p)) U(std::forward<Args>(args)...);\n    }\n};\n\n// World state\nstruct World {\n    std::vector<Material> materials;\n    std::vector<ElementStatic, FirstTouchAllocator<ElementStatic>> elements_static;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic, FirstTouchAllocator<ElementDynamic>> elements_dynamic;\n    std::vector<ElementDynamic, FirstTouchAllocator<ElementDynamic>> elements_dynamic_swap;\n};\n\n// Material type IDs\nconstexpr idx_t DEFAULT_MAT_ID = 0;\nconstexpr idx_t INFLOW_MAT_ID = 1;\nconstexpr idx_t OUTFLOW_MAT_ID = 2;\n\n// Pin each OpenMP thread to one CPU, spread evenly over the CPUs the process\n// is allowed to run on. This keeps threads from migrating between NUMA nodes,\n// so the first-touch page placement done during mesh construction stays local\n// to the thread that processes those elements during the simulation. The\n// OpenMP thread pool persists across parallel regions, so the pinning done\n// here carries over to all later regions. No-op (threads stay unpinned) if\n// querying the process affinity mask fails.\nvoid bindThreadsForNumaLocality() {\n    cpu_set_t proc_mask;\n    if (sched_getaffinity(0, sizeof(proc_mask), &proc_mask) != 0) {\n        return;\n    }\n    std::vector<int> cpus;\n    for (int c = 0; c < CPU_SETSIZE; ++c) {\n        if (CPU_ISSET(c, &proc_mask)) {\n            cpus.push_back(c);\n        }\n    }\n    if (cpus.empty()) {\n        return;\n    }\n    #pragma omp parallel proc_bind(spread)\n    {\n        const size_t t = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_thread_num());\n        const size_t nt = static_cast<size_t>(omp_get_num_threads());\n        const int cpu = cpus[std::min(t * cpus.size() / nt, cpus.size() - 1)];\n        cpu_set_t mask;\n        CPU_ZERO(&mask);\n        CPU_SET(cpu, &mask);\n        sched_setaffinity(0, sizeof(mask), &mask); element state\n                ElementDynamic& elem_write = write_buf[i];\n                elem_write.current_energy = elem_dyn.current_energy + total_flux;\n                elem_write.total_flux = std::numeric_limits<val_t>::max();\n    \n    for (const auto& elem : world.elements_dynamic) {\n        energy_sum += elem.current_energy;\n        flux_sum += elem.total_flux;\n        energy_max = %.12f\\n\", energy_sum);\n    printf(\"  Flux sum: %.2f\\n\", flux_sum);\n    printf(\"  Energy range: [%.6f, %.6f]\\n\", energy_min, energy_max);\n    \n    // Check for numerical issues\n !std::isfinite(energy_min)) {\n        printf(\"  ERROR: Energy extrema are not finite\\n\");\n        return false;\n    }\n    \n    printf(\"  Validation: PASSED\\n\");\n    return true;\n}\n\n// Compute a simple hash of the results for verification\nuint64_t computeHash(const std::vector<ElementDynamic, FirstTouchAllocator<ElementDynamic>>& elements) {\n    uint64_t hash = 0;\n    for (size_t i = 0; i < elements.size(); ++i) {\n        // Simple hash combining energy and flux values\n        const uint64_t* e_ptr = reinterpret_cast<const uint64_t*>(&elements[i].current_energy);\n {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-fable-5","id":"msg_011CejkXSzqUB9mor9vKtN31","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Done. The benchmark in `./unstructured/` is now parallelized with OpenMP, producing bit-identical results to the serial code at every thread count, with a ~30x speedup on this 2-socket, 128-core machine (2048×2048 grid, 50 iters: 2065 ms serial → ~60 ms parallel).\n\n**Changes in `unstructured.cpp`:**\n- `runSimulation` runs all iterations inside a single `#pragma omp parallel` region (one fork/join total instead of one per iteration). The element-update loop is a static-schedule `omp for`; each thread then swaps its `firstprivate` read/write buffer pointers after the loop's implicit barrier, so no extra synchronization is needed. A final vector swap for odd iteration counts preserves the original semantics of `world.elements_dynamic` holding the result. Per-element arithmetic order is unchanged, so results are bitwise identical to the original.\n- The mesh initialization and connectivity loops in `buildSquare2D` are parallelized with the same static schedule, which doubles as NUMA first-touch placement matching the simulation's work distribution.\n- Added a `FirstTouchAllocator` (default-init instead of value-init) so `vector::resize` doesn't zero the arrays on the main thread — without it, all pages landed on NUMA node 0 and capped scaling at ~178 ms.\n- Added `bindThreadsForNumaLocality()`, called at the start of `main`, which pins each OpenMP thread to one CPU spread across the process's allowed CPU set via `sched_setaffinity` (plain Linux/glibc, no new dependency). This was necessary because unpinned threads migrate across sockets and destroy first-touch locality (294 ms vs 59 ms), and libgomp ignores `proc_bind` when no place list is set in the environment. It degrades gracefully to a no-op if the affinity query fails, and respects any externally imposed CPU mask.\n\n**`CMakeLists.txt`:** added `find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED COMPONENTS CXX)` and linked `OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX`. The benchmark in `./unstructured/` is now parallelized with OpenMP, producing bit-identical results to the serial code at every thread count, with a ~30x speedup on this 2-socket, 128-core machine (2048×2048 grid, 50 iters: 2065 ms serial → ~60 ms parallel).\n\n**Changes in `unstructured.cpp`:**\n- `runSimulation` runs all iterations inside a single `#pragma omp parallel` region (one fork/join total instead of one per iteration). The element-update loop is a static-schedule `omp for`; each thread then swaps its `firstprivate` read/write buffer pointers after the loop's implicit barrier, so no extra synchronization is needed. A final vector swap for odd iteration counts preserves the original semantics of `world.elements_dynamic` holding the result. Per-element arithmetic order is unchanged, so results are bitwise identical to the original.\n- The mesh initialization and connectivity loops in `buildSquare2D` are parallelized with the same static schedule, which doubles as NUMA first-touch placement matching the simulation's work distribution.\n- Added a `FirstTouchAllocator` (default-init instead of value-init) so `vector::resize` doesn't zero the arrays on the main thread — without it, all pages landed on NUMA node 0 and capped scaling at ~178 ms.\n- Added `bindThreadsForNumaLocality()`, called at the start of `main`, which pins each OpenMP thread to one CPU spread across the process's allowed CPU set via `sched_setaffinity` (plain Linux/glibc, no new dependency). This was necessary because unpinned threads migrate across sockets and destroy first-touch locality (294 ms vs 59 ms), and libgomp ignores `proc_bind` when no place list is set in the environment. It degrades gracefully to a no-op if the affinity query fails, and respects any externally imposed CPU mask.\n\n**`CMakeLists.txt`:** added `find_package(OpenMP REQUIRED COMPONENTS CXX)` and linked `OpenMP::OpenMP_CXX`.
